# Gädlä Abunä Yonas Zä-Bur, Eritrean saint of the 15th century

**Author**: Tänśə’a Krəstos  
**Series**: PO Tome 53  
**Columns**: pp. 318–468  

[p. 318] {#po.53.p318}
በስመ' ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ፡ ዘገብሮ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ በቃሉ ። ወበስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልድ" ፡ ዘገብሮ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ በአምሳሉ፤ ። ወበስመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ እሉ ። ዘአጽንዖ ፣ ለአዳም ፡ በኀይሉ ። አብ ፡ወወልድ ፡ወመንፈስ ፡ቅዱስ ፣እምሥላሴሆሙ ፡እለ ፡፣ኢይትከፈሉ ። ወእምህላዌሆሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይሴሰሉ ። ለክብረ ፡ ሥላሴሆሙ ፡ ሰጊድ፤ ፡ ይደሉ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። አሜን ፣ ወአሜን ። ንንግር" ፡ ዜኖ ፡ ጊሩቱ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ አቡነ ፡ዮናስ ፣ ፅንቁ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ ዘኢዝሕለ ፡ በርስሐተ ፡ ጌጋይ ። ኮከብ ፣ ብሩህ ፡ ተላዌ ፡ ፀሓይ ። ዘያዋኪ ፡ በአየረ፤ ፡ ሰማይ ።
1 In the name of God the Father who made Adam by his word (Gen 1:26). And in the name of God the Son who made Adam in his likeness and in the name of God the Paraclete, their Spirit who strengthened Adam by his might. Father, Son and Holy Spirit. They are not divided from their Trinity, and do not abandon their essence. Worship is due to the glory of their Trinity, forever and ever. Amen and Amen Let us tell the news of the goodness of the chosen man of God, our father Yonas, He is a pearl that did not become rusty by the dirt of trespass. Radiant star, follower of the sun. Who shines in the air of the sky. Ear of wheat that the heat of summer did not scorch. May the blessing of his prayer be with us, amen.

### Birth, childhood and call of Abuna Yonas
ሠዊተ ፡ ስርናይ ፡ ዘኢየመጽለዎ' ፡ ሐሩረ ፡ ሐጋይ ። በረከተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ" ፡ ምስሌነ' ፡ አሜን ። ወሀሎ ፡ ፳ ብእሲ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ቡር ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወስሙ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወስመ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ኅሪተ'' ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወክልኤ ሆሙ፤፤! ፣ ፈራህያነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወወለዱ ፡ ደቀ ፡ ወአዋልደ ። ወአሐዱ" ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይርዒ'' ፡ አባግዐ ፡ ተምህረ ፡ መዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ድ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ መልአክ ፡እንዘ ፡ ንኡስ ፡፣ ውእቱ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ መጽኦ" ፡ ኅሊና ፣ ዘይብል ፣ ምንተ ፡ ይበቀዓኒ ፡ ንብረተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ እመ ፡ ረከብክዎ ፡ የሐልፈኒ ፡ ወእመ ፣፡ ኃጣእክዎ ፡ የሐዝነኒ"'' ። ወባ ሕቱ ፡ ተሊወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይኀይሰኒ ።
2 There was a man in the country of Bur whose name was Hagara Masql. His name was Newaya Jgzi’s and the name of his wife Harita Maryam. Both were God fearing. They begot male and female (children) and one of them by the name Habta Sgzi’s, while he was shepherding the sheep learned the Psalm of David 70 (Ps 70:1.5-6) from an angel while he was young. After this a thought came to him, saying: “What will profit to me the life of this world, (even) if | get it, it will pass away and if | miss it, it will cause grief to me. For me though it is better following God”.

### Abuna Yonas becomes monk
ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ኀደገ"! ፡ አባግዒሁ ፣ ወሖረ ፡ ፍኖቶ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመ ርሖ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ደብር ፡ እምአድባረ ፡ ሠራዌ'' ፡ እንተ ፡ስማ ፡ ደብረ ፡ሲና ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ እስመ ፡
3 Having said this he abandoned his sheep and went his way as the Holy Spirit was leading him and conducted him to a one of the monasteries of Sarawe by the name Dabra Sina, the monastery of our father Demyanos because he


[p. 320] {#po.53.p320}
ፀ. እኅወ ፡ እሙ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና' ፡ ወሰመዮ ፡ ስሞ ፣ ዮኖስ ፡፣ ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ወጠነ ፡ ገድለ ፡፣ ወመነነ' ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ተድላሂ ። ወወጠነ ፣ ገድለ' ፡ በቀዊም ። ወአይበሰ ፡ አባሉ ፡ በጾም ። ወሶበ ፡ ይሁብዎ ፡ መብልዐ ፣ ይነሥእ ፣ ከመ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ ወይሁቦሙ ፡ ለነዳያን ። በኅቡእ' ።
was the brother of his mother. He clothed him with the monastic garments and named him Yonas and from then he began the gadl and renounced the pleasure of the world. He began the gadl by standing. He dried his body parts by fasting. And when they would give him food, he took (it) as if he ate and gave it to the poor in secret.

### Jesus Christ appears to Abuna Yonas and makes a promise to him
ወበአሐቲ፡ ፡ ሌሊት" ፡ 'እምነ ፡ ለያልይ፡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል" ፡ በዊኦ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ቀላይ ። ወመጽአ" ፡ ኀቤሁ ፣፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በደመና ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወረ ከቦ ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ አንብዕ'' ፡ ውዑይ"! ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም" ፡ ለከ ፡፣ ዮናስ" ፡ ገባሬ ፡ ፈቃድየ ፡ መሐልኩ" ፣ ለከ ፣ በርእስየ ፡ በሥምረተ" ፡ አቡየ ፡፣ ወበሥም ረትየ'" ። ወበሥምረተ ፣ የቅዱስ ፣ መንፈስየ" ። ኩሎ ፡ ዘሰምዐ" ፡ ቃለ ፡ መጽሐፍዋየ ፣ እምአፉከ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ዘዐቀበ ። ወነሥአ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ በእደዊከ ፣ ወቆብዐ ፣ ወኀሜለተ"' ፡ ወወሀብኩከ፤፤ ፡ ዓሥራተ” ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፲፻ ፡ ፻፻ወ፳፻ ፣ ፻፻ወ፳ወ፳ ፡ ፻፻እለ ፣ ይነግሥ ፣ ምስሌከ ፡ ወአለሰ"' ፡ ይድኅኑ ፡ በጸውዖ ፣፡ ስምከ ፣ ወተማሕፅኖ"' ፡፣ በጸሎትከ” ፣ መሐርኩ” ፡ ለከ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ጭልቀ”" ፡ ከመ ፡ ነጠብጣበ ፡ ክረምት ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ ወዘአጽሐፈ” ፣ መጽሐፈ ፣ ገድልከ ፡ ወዘጸሐፈ ፡ ለሊሁ” ፡ ተአሚኖ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ይድኃን ፡ በእንቲአከ ። እመኒ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ኃጢአቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኳኳ ፡ አፃዓድዎ ፡ ከመ ፣ በረድ" ። ወለከ ፡ ወሀብኩከ ፣ መባሕተ ፡ ትምሀር"' ፡ ወትገሥጽ ፡ አባግዕየ ። ዘንተ ፡ ይቤሎ ፣ መድኃኒነ" ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮኖስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ማእከለ ፣ ቀላይ ። ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዓምደ" ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹልሎ ፣
4 One night while he was fighting the spiritual fight entering in the middle of a depth, our Lord came to him in a cloud of the heaven and found him with hot tears. He told him: “Peace to you, Yonas, doer of my willl | have sworn to you by myself, with my Father's consent, with my consent and with the consent of my Holy Spirit! All who hear the word of my book from your mouth, and that hearing keeps it and takes the askema by your hands, and the monastic cap and mantle, | have given you as a tithe in the measure of thousands of thousands and three hundred and sixty five thousands. Those who will reign with you and those who will be saved by invoking your name and by confiding in your prayer, | am going to pardon always for your sake like the drops of the rainy season. Everyone that celebrates your memory and lets the book of your gadl be written, or writes it himself, trusting in your prayer, will be saved for your sake. If his sin is like a scarlet | will whiten it like snow (Isa 1:18). And to you | give the authority to teach and to admonish my sheep”. Our Saviour told this to our father Yonas while he was in the middle of the depth and our Lord was standing like a pillar of light, while a cloud from heaven was floating over him (Ex 14:24; Num 9:15-22).

### Abuna Yonas responds to the promise of Jesus Christ
ደመና ፡ ሰማይ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ በልብ ፡ ትሑት ፡ ወበድኩም ፡ ልሳን ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኪዳንሰ ፡ ዘወሀብከኒ ፡ ይትአኩት ፡ ስምከ ፡ ወይትባረክ ፡ መንግሥትከ ። ወበእንተሰ ፡ ዘትቤለኒ ፣ ገሥጽ ፡ ወመሀር” ። አኮ ፡ ለዘ ፣ ከማየ ፡፣ ዘኢየአምር” ፡ ሙባእየ ፣ ወሙፃእየ ፡ ወነዳየ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ አነ ፡ ወአል ብየ ፣፡ከሂል" ፣ ለምህሮ ። ኅድገኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ እብኪ"' ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ።
5 Our father Yonas replied to him with a humble heart and with a soft language. He told our Lord: “As for the pact you gave me, may your name be thanked and your kingdom blessed, but with regard to what you told me ‘admonish and teach’, it is not for someone like me who does not know my entering and my going out and | am poor in knowledge and do not have the capacity to teach. Leave me, O Lord to cry for my sin!” (1 Kings 3:7).

### Jesus Christ speaks again and departs
ወአውሥኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢለበውከኑ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እቤሎ ፡ ለሙሴ ። መኑ ፡፣ ወሀቦ' ፡ ለእጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ አፈ" ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይንብብ ። ወመኑ ፣ ረሰዮ' ፣ ለእንስሳ ፡፣ በሀመ፤ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ አነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላ ክከ' ፡ አምላከ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ ዘብየ ፣ ሥልጣን፤' ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ' ፡ አእሚርየ ፡ ወሀብኩ ፡ ንዋይየ ፣ ለዘሠምረት ፡ ነፍስየ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ።
6 Our Lord replied to him and told him: “Did you not realize what | told Moses? Who gave the mouth to the son of man (Ex 4:11) that he might speak? And who made the animal dumb? Was it not |, the Lord your God, the God of Abraham, Isaac and Jacob, | who have the power over all, knowingly I gave my riches to those with whom my soul took delight’. Having said this, our Lord ascended to heaven.

### Abuna Yonas leaves for Damot
ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ርሑቀ" ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግበ ፡፣ ኢይሰማፅ' ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ዳሞት ፡ በከመ ፣ ጐየ ፣ ዮናስ ፡፣ መኩስየ'" ፣ ስሙ ፡፣ እምገጸ'' ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወለ እመ ፡ ነገርነ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ዮናኖስ ፡ ይነውኅ ፡ ነገር'' ። ወከማሁ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ርሑቀ'' ፡ ብሔረ ፡ በከመ ፡ ንቤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመስሎ ዘያመሥጥ" ፡ እምዕበይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነገርዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ከመ ፣ ፈለሰ ፣ አእሚሮ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ኢትሕዝኑ" ፡ በእንተ ፡ እጉክሙ ። እስመ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ አምላክየ" ፡ አነብሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መን በርከ ፣ ለዮናስ ፡ ወልድከ ፡ ወእሬስያ ፡ ዕዝዝተ'' ፡ ለበረከቱ" ። ወይሰማዕ ፡ ትምህርቱ ፡ በመኣዝነ” ፣ ዓለም ፡ ፪ ። ለሊሁ ፡ አምላክየ" ፡ ወሰዶ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ ወይመይጦ" ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይትዓዶ ፡ እምቃሉ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብ ሐት ፡ ይድሉ
7 Having heard this, our father Yonas migrated to a far off country where no news about him would be heard and he reached the edge of Ethiopia whose name is Damot, in the same way his namesake Jonah fled from the face of God (Jon 1:3). If we told all that happened concerning Jonah the discourse would be long. Likewise, this father migrated to a far off country, as we said, thinking that he would run away from greatness. When they told our father Damyanos that he migrated, informed by the Holy Spirit, he told the brothers: “Do not be grieved about your brother because my God has told me: ‘I will place your disciple Yonas upon your seat, | will make strong his blessing, his teaching will be heard in the four corners of the world. It is my God himself that took him by his will and by his will he will bring him back, for there is nothing that will encroach on his word. To him praise is duel”.

### Arrival at Damot: impact with the monastic community
ንግባእኬ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጾታ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘጊዜ ፣ ፍልሰቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ እስከ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ዳሞት ፡ ወቦአ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ፅሪኖ ። ሠኖያነ” ፣ ግዕዝ ፣ ወያፈቅሩ ፡ ጽሙና ፡፣ ወርእዮ” ፣ ሥርዓቶሙ ፣ ወሥነ” ፡ ግዕዞሙ ፣ አፍቀሮሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለአኃዊሁ ፡ ንንበር” ፡ ምስለ ፡ እሉ ፡ ሠኖያነ ፡፣ ግዕዝ ፡ ወንኅድግ” ፣ ሥምረተነ ። ወአውሥኡ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፣ አኃዊሁ” ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ እፎ ፡ ነኀድጎ” ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፣ ቀላየ ፡ የውሆ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ኃጣእነ” ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ አኮኑ" ፡ በአገብሮ ፡ ኅሊና" ፡ ኀደግናሁ” ፣ ወእምአመ” ፡ ኀደግናሁ” ፣ ኢረከብነ ፡ ዘከማሁ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንንበር”"' ፡ ዝየ ፣ ይጌይሰነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አበው ፣ ዘመንኩሰ ፣ መኒኖ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ይሖር ፣ ርሑቀ ፡ ብሔረ" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢይሰምዕ።" ፡ ዜና ፡ አዝማዲሁ ። በዝየሰ” ፣ ለእመ ፡ ሞትነ ፡ ኢየሐዝኑ" ፡ አዝማዲነ ፡ ወንሕነኒ ፣ ኢነሐዝን"' ፣ በእንቲአሆሙ ።
8 Let us go back into the exposition of the account of the time of his migration. And thence he arrived at the country of Damot and entered into the house of the community that lived in equality, with good habits, and they loved hard work. Observing their regulation and the beauty of their habits, he loved them and told his brothers: “Let us live with these ones who have good habits and let us abandon our community!”. His brothers who came with him replied and told him: “How can we abandon our father Damyanos, the depth of meekness? What did we lack from him? Was it not by a coercion of mind that we abandoned him? And since we abandoned him we did not find anyone like him”. He told them: “Let us stay here, it is better for us, for the holy fathers said: ‘Let the one that has become a monk, renouncing the world, go to a far off country in which he will not hear news of his relatives. If we die here, our relatives will not grieve and we will not grieve on their account”.

### Abunda Yonas’s companions decide to go back to Sarawe
። ወይቤልዎ ፣ አኃዊሁ ፡፣ ንሕነሰ' ፡ ኢንነብር ፡ ዝየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ያፈቅሩነ ፡ አበዊነ ፣ ወአኃዊነ' ፡ ወንሕነኒ ፡ ናፈቅሮሙ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፣ ለአኃዊሁ ፡ አነኒ ፡ ‹አፈቅሮሙ ፣ ከማክሙ፤ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ኢይትከሀለኒ፤ ፡ መጺአ ፡ ምስሌክሙ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ምንት" ፣ ነገሩ ፣ በዘኢይትከሀለከ ፡ መጺአ ። ወይ ቤሎሙ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ተአምሩ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመስሎ፤' ፡ ዘያመሥጥ ፡ እምዕበይ ። ወተሰናዓልዎ ፡በ ወበከይዎ' ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ያነክሩ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አስተፋንወነ' ፡ ለዮም ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢንተረአይ" ፣ እምዝ ፡ ዳግመ'፤' ፣ አንተሂ"፤ ፡ ምዉት ፡ በኀቤነ ፣ ወንሕነኒ ፡ ምውታን ፡ በኀቤከ ።
9 His brothers told him: “But we will not stay here because our fathers and brothers love us and we too love them”. And our father Yonas told his brothers: “I also love them like you, but it is not possible for me to come with youl”. And they told: “What is the reason for which it is impossible for you to come?”. He told them: “What do you know about what is in my heart?”. He told them this thinking that he would escape from greatness. They bade him farewell, and cried over him as if he were dead, so much that the people of the country were amazed. They told him: “Accompany us along today’s journey, because from now on we will not see each other again, you are dead for us and we too are dead for you!”.

### Negotiations to persuade Abuna Yonas to return to Sarawe
ወውእተ" ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጌረወ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ ቃለ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ወዘእንበሌየሰ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘትክሉ ፡ ገቢረ ። ወሖረ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ የ'ይእተ ፡ ዕለተ" ፡ ወቤቱ ፡ ኅቡረ ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በከዩ ፡ ሳዕሌሁ ፡ ወይ ቤልዎ ፡ ለዮምኒ ፡ ኢትኅድገነ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ መዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብልዎ ፡ ለለጌሠሙ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ፡።፡" እስከ ፡ ፳ ፡ ዕለት ። ወካዕበ ፡ በከዩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ምንተኑ" ፡ ንዜኑ'' ፡ ለብሔርነ" ፡ የምንተ ፡ን ብሎሙ” ፣ ሞትከኑ”" ፣ ንብሎሙ ፡ ዘኢሞትከ ። ወለእመ ፡ ንቤሎሙ ፣ ኃደ ግናሁ ፣ ንከውን ፣ ሠሐቀ"፤' ፡ ወስላቀ ፣ ለቅሩብነ፡! ፡ ወለርጉቅነ፡' ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ያፈቅረከ ።
10 At that time his heart was moved, so that the word of God which says: But without me there is nothing you can do (Jn 15:5) might be fulfilled. And he went with them that day and they spent the night together. And in the following day they cried over him again and told him: “For today too, do not abandon us!”. Again he went with them as every morning they were telling him this, and he went with them until the fifth day. Again they cried over him and told him: “What will we report to our country, what shall we tell them? Will we tell them that you died while you did not die? And if we tell them that we have abandoned you, we will become a laughing stock and ridiculous to the near and to the far ones, because everyone loves you”.

### Abuna Yonas capitulates
ወዘንተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብልዎ ፡ በከዩ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኅድ ጉኒ” ፣ ኢትስብሩኒ” ፡ ልብየ” ፣ አሐተ ፡ ግበሩ ፡ ሊተ" ፡ ናንብር ፡ መስቀለ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ወንጸሊ” ፣ ወዘፈቀደ” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለይኩን ። ወአን በሩ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡፣ ታቦት ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ በሥምረተ" ፡ ጵ" ፣ ልብ ፡ ወወፅአ" ፡ ዘይብል ፡ እቱ ፡ ብሔረከ” ፣ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ፈቃደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ፈቃድየ ፡ ወአተው ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ ግበ ፡ መምህሮሙ። ፣ ወተቀበልዎሙ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ተቀብሎተ ፡ አኃዊሆሙ ።
11 They wept bitterly as they were telling him this. He told them: “Leave me alone! Do not break my heart! (Acts 21:13). Do one thing for me. Let us put a cross upon the altar and let us pray, let it be what God wants!”. They placed a cross upon the altar and they prayed together, with the unity of one heart and (a voice) came out that said: “Go back to your country!”. And he said: “Let the will of God be done and not my will” (Mt 26:39) and they returned to their country, to their master and their brothers welcomed them with a good welcome.


[p. 326] {#po.53.p326}
ወሶበ ፡ አተወ ፡ እምፍልሰቱ ፡ ተሠይመ፤ ፣ ቀሲሰ' ፡፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በሥምረቱ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ' ፡ አሜን ። ንግባእኬ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ጾታ፤' ፣ ነገር ፡ ወናየድዕ' ፡ ዘከመ ፡፣ ተሠይመ ፡ መምህረ ፡ ወኢነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፣ መምህሩ ፣ እስከ ፡ ፲ ፡ ዓመት ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ወሰከ' ፡ ገድለ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ገድል ፡ ጸመ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይጠግዕፅ ፡ ማእሱ" ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዓጽሙ ፡ ወቆመ ፡፣ እስከ ፣ የሐብጥ" ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወይዜምር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አርምሞ ። ወበጸመ ፡ ቫ ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ይትጋደል ፡ ጽኑዐ ፣ ወኢይጥዕም ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ምድር ። ወበሣልስት ፣ ፅለት ፣ ይጥዕም ፡ ፳ ፡ ኅጠተ ፡ እምፍሬ ፡ ዳዕሮ ፡፣ ወእመአኮ ፡ ድ"! ፡ ፍሬ ፣
Abunda Yonas ordained priest upon his return to Sarawe boosts his ascetic zeal 12 And when he returned from his migration, he was ordained priest by the benevolence of God, may his prayer and blessing be with us, amen. Let us go back into the exposition of the account and we tell that he was appointed master but he did not sit on the chair of his master for ten years. From then on, he increased gadl upon gad, he fasted till his skin stuck to his bone, he stood till his legs were swollen and was singing without interruption. During the forty days of holy Lent he did penance severely and did not taste the seed of the earth. On the third day he would eat five grains of sycamore’s fruit or else five fruits of ‘aye.

13 He prostrated day and night without rest until he completed the one hundred and fifty Psalms of David, the Hymns of the Prophets, and the Songs of Solomon; until he completed the Praises of Mary. These (prayers he prayed) twice and did not rest from prostrating until his sweat became like drops. When he completed this, he stood in his usual position and did not rest on a place where he could lean. He would not hold a stick and would read the Book of the Hours of our holy fathers and the books of the prophets and apostles. He would not rest day and night, (even) one hour from reading: he lived like this during the forty days of holy Lent. we went though, it was not during the days of fasts but on other days and | did not know that he lived in that cell. One of the monks who was in that cave told me: ‘When he completed his prayer, animals and beasts came to him and would be blessed by him and lived serving him’. | told him: ‘Father who is he?’. He told me: ‘What is your worry? God knows him’. | told him: ‘No! Tell me his name, father, that | may entrust myself to his prayer’. He told me: ‘Will you keep it in your heart until you tell it in due time?’. | told him: ‘Yes! | will do your will that you are telling me’. He told me: ‘It is me but do not tell before the exit of my soul”.

15 And again, one day he went out to the desert taking one of his disciples at the time of his prayers of intercession and told that disciple, while going out from the town: “Do not leave the water pot and the trestle because there are those who need it’. And he took the water pot and the trestle and arrived at his cell of old. He ordered him to draw water by that water pot, each evening. He told him: “My son do not interrupt not even a day and place it before me together with the trestle”. That disciple lived doing like this.

16 While that disciple was doing (this), a thought came to him and he thought saying this: “What is the use of this water that | bring? | don't see anyone drinking it and | don’t know where it is poured out”. This thing became hard for his mind. Having said this he slept, according to his habit, opposite to our father, but that one was praying standing according to his habit. Before sleep did visit that disciple, while his eyes were watching and his face was uncovered, a lion came before the vigil of the night, agitating his tail and ears and reached where this blessed father of ours Yonas was praying; it prostrated in front of him and rolled himself below him and licked the dust off his feet.

18 And again, one day when our father was in Hanaba, lions overwhelmed the people of that country in those days and were killing those they met. One night a disciple rose from his sleeping place and went where our father Yonas was praying before mid-night and found him entertaining a lion and was measuring him with his hands and was extracting evil from his teeth. When that disciple arrived unexpectedly, he flew with might and passed and reached where he wanted (to be). When this happened, our father was angry with that disciple and told him: “My son, why did you do like this? Your coming is not good because to every deed there is a time (Eccl 3:1), and everything in its order”,
ቦቱ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ በበሥርዓቱ" ፣ ወሶበ ፡ "ይቤሎ ፡ ከመዝ'! ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገነየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፣ ወአምዣጥ'! ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ አባ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ ሥረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እስመ ፡ በኢ ያእምሮ'' ፡ ገበርኩ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይመስለኒ ፡ ዘእጠብብ ፡ አበድኩ ፡ ወተዘለፍኩ" ፡ በእበድየ" ። ርዕደ" ፣ ብረኪሁ'' ፡ ወኢሀለወት" ፣ ልቡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰረረ” ፣ ኅሊናሁ” ፡ ወኃጥአ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ሥረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ የአክለኒ"' ፡ ዘለፋየ ፡ እምዮም ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይቤሎ ፣ ጌረወ" ፡ ልቡ" ፡ አቡነ ፣ ዮናስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ወልድየ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ዘእቤለከ ፡ አኮ ፡ ኪያከ ፡ ጸሊእየ” ፡ አላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታእምር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወኢይርከብከ ፡ እኩይ” ። ወዘ ንተ ፡፣ ብሂሎ ፡ ወረቀ ፡ ምራቆ ፡ ወቀብኦ ፡ ፍጽሞ ፡ በአርአያ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወጥዕየ ፡ አባሉ ፡ ወሰሰለ” ፡ ፍርሃት፤' ፣ ዘላዕሌሁ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምስካቡ” ።
19 When our father spoke to him like this, that disciple bowed to him and paid his respect to him. He told him: “Father! Father! | have done wrong, forgive me for | have acted in my ignorance and while thinking that | was behaving wisely, | became foolish and | have been reprimanded because of my foolishness”. His knees trembled and his heart was not in him, his thought flew and his essence came short and he said: “Forgive me! The reprimand | received is sufficient for me from today until eternity!”. 20 As he was telling this, the heart of our father Yonas was moved and told that disciple: “Do not be afraid, my son! | am telling you this not because | hate you, but in order that you may know what is good and that evil may not meet you”. Having said this he spat his spittle and anointed his forehead in the guise of the sign of the cross and his body part became healthy; the fear that was upon him departed and he returned to his sleeping place.

### Disclosure of the monk's identity and of his prophecy
ወውእቱ ፣ መነኮስ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኃያል ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወመስተጋድል” ፣ ወስሙኒ ፡ ኢዩኤል ፣ በአማን ፣ መሥምር ፡ ለአኅው ፡ ወቅኑይ ፡ ለአበው ። ወእንዘ ፣ የሐውር"' ፡ ፍኖተ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፣ ይቤሎሙ'! ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፣ ለአኃው ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይበጽሕ ፡ እመውት ፡ በፍኖት ፣ ዳእሙ" ፣ አሐውር" ፡ በፍቅር ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሞተ ፡ በፍኖት ። ወበጊዜ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ቦእኩ ፣ ማኅደረ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፣ ምስሌነ” ፡ አሜን" ።
21 The monk that saw this, was a powerful, ascetic monk, and his name was lyyu’el, he was truly agreeable to brothers and submitted to the fathers. While travelling to Jerusalem, that monk told the brothers: “As for me, | will not reach (Jerusalem), | will die on the way, but | will go in love!”. As he said he died on the way. At the time of his death he said: “I have entered the dwelling of the Father, of the Son and of the Holy Spirit”. May his prayer and blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 332] {#po.53.p332}

### Again Abuna Yonas and lions
ወውእቱሰ' ፡፣ አንበሳ ፡ ዘወፅአ፤ ፡ እማኅደሩ ፡ ለአቡነ' ፡ ዮኖስ ፡ ጥሕረ' ፡ በኀይል ፡ ወጣህሩኒ' ፡ ለደብር ፡ አኮሳ' ፡ አርዓደ ፡ ሰብአ' ፡ ወአፍርሀ ፡ እን ስሳ ። ምንተ ፡ ንብሎ ፡፣ ለዘከመዝ" ፡ መድምም ፡ ዘአርአየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፣ ለባሴ ፣፡ ገዳም ። ወዘከመ ፣ ተዛውዐ ፡-ምስለ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ግሩም ። ወተሰምዐ'' ፡ ዜኖ'' ፡ ጊሩቱ ፡ ውስተ"'' ፡ አድያም" ፡ በረከተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ" ። አሜን" ።
22 As for that lion that came out from the dwelling of our father Yonas, he roared with might and his roaring agitated the monastery and caused trembling to the people and frightened the animals. What do we say to such a wonder that our father Yonas, wearer of the desert, showed? On how he entertained a terrifying lion. The fame of his goodness was heard in the region. May his prayer and blessing be with us, Amen.

### Abuna Yonas ministers in the region of ‘Asam and Zarafta
ወካዕበ ፡፣ ስምዑ ፡ ንንግርክሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አድያመ ፡ ዓሰም" ፡ ወዘረፍታ ፣ አመ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ለስብከት ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ በዕለተ ፣ ዐርብ ፡ዘበአተ'' ፡ ድራረ" ፡ ጾም ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ" ፡ ሀገር ፡፣ እምአህጉረ” ፣ ዓሰም?'፤ ። ወተቀበልዎ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ቴራነ ፡ ዘመድ ፡ እለ ፡ ይብልዎሙ ፡ ባሲሐ ፡ አግኔ” ፣ ወተቀበልዎ" ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ተቀብሎተ ፡ ፅድ” ፡ ወአንስት" ፡ ወይጻዓቁ”" ፡ ለተቀብሎቱ ፡ ወለተአምኖቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተሰቅሉ"' ፡ በሰሚዐ" ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ወት ምህርቱ ፡ ወርኢይሰ” ፡ ኢርእይዎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አሜሃ ። ወበቀዳሚት" ፡ ሰን በት ፡ ወዓለ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሜህር" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይጥዓም ፡ እክለ ፡ ዘእንበለ" ፡ ንስ ቲት ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ በረከት ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይኩኖ ፡ ጾም" ። ወበከመ ፡ ንቤ ፡ ወዓለ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሜህር” ፣ ገባርያነ ፡፣ ሠናይ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይድኅኑ ፡ ወገበርተ ፡ ዐመፃ ፣ ዘከመ” ፣ ይትኩነኑ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ይሰቀሉ ፡ ለአጽምኦቱ" ፡ እስመ ፡ ሐዋዝ" ፡ ሥነ” ፣ ትምህርቱ ። ወሰርከ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ለፀቢሐ” ፡ እሑድ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ በከመ ፡ ያለምድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢይነውም ፡ መዓልተ" ፡ ወሌሊተ" ፡
23 Hearken again! We will tell you what happened in the region of ‘Asam and Zarafta when he went for preaching. He arrived with his disciples at one of the towns of ‘Asam on a Friday before the supper of fast. People of a good family, called Siha Agane welcomed him with a good welcome, men and women were pressing to welcome and to greet him because they were lifted by what they heard regarding the fame of his teaching (Lk 19:48), but as for seeing him, they did not see him except then. On the first Sabbath he spent the day teaching, without tasting food except a little bread of blessing so that it may not seem a fast to him. As we said, he spent the day teaching that the doers of good would be saved while the evil doers would be condemned, and all would be lifted to listen to him for the beauty of his teaching was sweet. On the Sabbath evening before Sunday morning he spent the night praying as was his custom, for he did not sleep either day or night on the day of Sunday. While in ‘Asam Abunda Yonas sees the death of a disciple in Sarawe
ለዕለተ" ፡ እሑድ" ። ወቤተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወያውሕዝ" ፣ አንብዐ ፡ እምአፅይን ቲሁ ። ወይቤሉ ፡፣ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ምንተ ፣ ጥነ ፡ ዝአቡነ" ፣ ወምንተ ፡ ሐዘን ፣ ወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ዘከመዝ ፡ ብካየ" ፡ ወሰቆቃወ ፡ ዘንሰምዕ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸብሐ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ጸሎተ ፣ እምነግህ" ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እ፻ሰዓት ፡ በከመ ፣ ያለምድ ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፡ ሎሙ ፡፣ ማእደ ፡ ወአበየ” ፣ ጥዒመ ። ወይቤልዎ" ፣ ኦአባ ፣ ለምንት ፡ ዘኢትጥዕም ፣፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፣ ከመዝኑ ፡ እንጋ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በአሐቲ"' ፡ ዕለት" ፡ ቦዘ" ፡ ይበልዕ ፣ እክለ ፡ ወቦ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ መሬተ ።
24 He spent the night weeping bitterly and shedding tears from his eyes. His disciples said: “What happened to this father of ours and what sort of grief has fallen upon him that we hear such a weeping and lamentation from him?”. When it became morning, he prayed from morning until three o'clock as he used to do. They brought to them food but he refused to taste (it). They told him: “Oh father, why don’t you taste?”. He told them: “Is man(kind) like this? On the same day someone eats food and somebody else

eats earth!”. His disciples told him: “Where has happened like that, o father?”. He told them: “Why do you worry? You will know it afterwards”. He told them this on account of the death of his disciple in Sarawe, and that was a chosen monk; he loved God and his master. At that time the one who died, while he was in Sarawe said: “Don't you see our father Yonas? His light is shining like the sun, sitting upon a radiant chair, he told me: ‘Well behaved, my son, you have defeated the world (Jn 16:28)!’. He told me this and moved on from me”. Look how astonishing is this! While he was in the land of ‘Asam, he knew what had happened, that his disciple had died in Sarawe! May his blessing be with us, amen. Abuna Yonas brings back to life from death Tanse’a Krestos, his would be biographer 25 Again hearken! We will report to you how he revived a dead man by his prayer when he was in Dabra Sahl, the place of his monastery. There was someone whose fellow student was ill of a deadly illness and nothing was left to him except a little breath. When that blessed father of ours saw him, he shed tears in mourning. He entered into his cell and locked himself in; he lifted up his thought to heaven. He shut his throat to drink and deprived his stomach of food and let a promise out from his mouth that he would not enter into the shadow of his house and he would not climb to the backrest of his sleeping place, that he would not give sleep to his eyes nor slumber to his eyelids, nor rest to his cheeks (Ps 131:2-4) until he made the word of his promise heard. With regard to this matter he constantly prayed, strengthened himself by standing, did not interrupt prostrating from Wednesday to Friday evening. From Friday evening until the Sunday Sabbath he prayed abundantly standing like a pillar. On the first hour of the day of Sunday that dead and wrapped person moved and said: “In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit’.

came to me and told me: ‘Do not fear! The Lord has revived you by the prayer of your master. It would have been much better for you if you lived with us, but your master did not leave you, go and take leave in peace!”. It is the one who was resurrected that told this, and he himself made the book of the gad! of our father Yonas to be written, becoming a witness of what he saw and his name is Tanse’a Krestos.

### Abuna Yonas rescues from death Muse, his blood brother

27 And it happened that one day among days, there was a priest with a sweet voice, brother of our father Yonas in the flesh and also in the spirit. He was ill of a deadly illness and his thought and spirit was drawn into heaven. They reported this to our father Yonas and having entered into his cell, he mourned and prayed saying: “O Lord do not darken the light of your sanctuary!”. When our father Yonas prayed like this, his prayer ascended to God.

28 The life of the one who died was given back by his prayer, the soul of the one who died was returned into his body. He told our father Yonas: “Father, what wrong did | do against you that you did not leave me to go to my eternal resting place to the houses of light where the vigilant angels go back and forth! Let me not enter where Satan is”. The one who rose (from death) and said this was our father Muse, brother of our father Yonas. After he rose from death, he lived for many years and rested on Tagamt 24. This is our father Yonas’s second miracle in resurrecting the dead. Abuna Yonas brings back to life a dead person for the third time

29 Let us go back again to telling the gadl of our father, the blessed Yonas. It happened that on one day when he went out to instruct the faithful according to his custom, he arrived at one of the towns of Sarawe. There was a man, there, who was drawing near death and his relatives seeing that our father Yonas had arrived, they carried him and brought him to the dwelling place of our father Yonas and told him: “Take! We brought him to you in the same way the relatives brought the cripple to the Lord Jesus” (Mt 9:2-7).

gadl, carried it upon his head and stood over the dead person. He said: “ask and implore you, Lord! Hear my prayer and accept my entreaty, you who accepted the entreaty of all our fathers who pleased you in their various eras, like them, hear my prayer of me your servant and heal the soul of this young man”. Having said this he spat his spittle on his hands and signed his body parts and told him: “Rise from your sleep! In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit”. The young man rose in might as if waking up from sleep (Ps 77:65). Our father Yonas thanked the name of his Creator and gave back alive that young man to his relatives. This became the third miracle of our father Yonas in resurrecting the dead.

When the locust fell over the fields those who were learning stood up from where they were learning, all those who had the capacity of expelling the locusts, with the cord by which they used to beat their oxen and with every vessel by which they would expel the locust.

34 Our father Yonas shouted to them and told them: “Did you not hear what the prophet Isaiah said about the Sabbath: ‘Do not raise your hands and your legs on the Sabbath to do every desire of yours and do not respond with a bad word by your mouth’ (Isa 58:13). Return and don't go until you see the power that God will do for you”. Having said this he watched the heaven and prayed and said: “Lord! You know that the heart of the sons of man is like ash and they don’t have the capacity for patience, because it says: The life of man is food and water (Sir 29:28 Eth., LXX 29:21)”.

35 Having said this he raised his hand, signed with the sign of the Cross and told it (the locust): “God is telling you: ‘Rise!’ and do not taste the crop and the grass of this town!”. That locust rose like smoke and flew over the airs until it was not visible to the eye and proceeded towards the west of Sarawe. It did not taste anything and the town was saved as that dreamer had told. They thanked and praised the Lord. This is a wonderful thing, astounding to the one hearing it.

was Gabra Maryam, who was living in the country of Egypt. An Egyptian from that country invited him so that he might receive from the blessing of his house. That Egyptian offered to him meat from the market place and told him: “Take, eat!”. That monk told that Egyptian: “As for me, | will not eat meat in this country because the habit of your country is not good (even) to recount, for you Christians eat what the Muslims butchered and the Muslims eat what the Christians butchered, taking a (single) word from Paul that says: Eat everything that you find in the market and do not ask (1 Cor 10:25) and because of this you err a lot. And now, what he called market place is the holy church and the meat are the faithful, the sheep of Christ's flock. And the eating is communion with them in the faith so that we do not disdain the faithful”. And that Egyptian told him: “If you are saying like this about the meat of the market place, eat then what | slaughtered. For | am a Christian like you”. He (the monk) told him: “All right! | will indeed eat what you slaughter”. He slaughtered a chicken, he cooked it nicely and gave it to him so that he might eat, and he ate and blessed him and slept.

Egypt 37 Our father Yonas appeared to him that night and came to him in great awe and in his hand was held a whip of fiery colour and he told him: “Gabra Maryam! Gabra Maryam!”. He told: “Here am | my father!”. He told him: “Why do you eat meat in this country?”. He told him: “Oh my father, did | not eat what a Christian slaughtered? But | did not eat meat of the market place”. He told him: “What Christianity has he got, since he is eating meat that the Muslims have slaughtered”. Having said this he was angry with him and beat him with a beating by that whip that was in his hand and his soul was tormented and wanted to exit from his body from the fatigue of the beating and his soul returned (only) with hardship.

38 When it became morning, he opened his body and saw where he was. beaten. It had become like the burning of fire in the likeness of milt and he showed that spot to his companions. He told them again that he had visited him and appeared to him, that he was angry with him and scourged him. Listening and seeing this they admired him much and were greatly astounded and all of those who heard said: “Let that admonition be (a lesson) to all of

us from now until eternity”. And many years after, when his brothers went down to the land of Egypt from Ethiopia, he told them all that took place He showed them that beating, which had turned into a coloured scar. And coming out, they too recounted this to the land of Ethiopia. Glory be to the Artist who showed to our father Yonas in Ethiopia what took place in Alexandria. To him submission with a hallelujah! Amen.

### Bilocation: from Ethiopia Abuna Yonas presides in incognito the
Eucharist in Jerusalem

39 And again, hear the report of the goodness of our blessed father Yonas of good σα! (2 Tim 4:7). While he was in the country of Ethiopia he mounted on the chariot of the Spirit and reached Jerusalem, he wore the priestly robes because it was the time of the Eucharist and he prepared every sacred vessel and undertook the priestly service; he began, and in a melodious chant he said: “The one Father is Holy, the one Son is Holy, the one Spirit is Holy”. And as he completed worthily the whole Eucharistic celebration, while our Lord Jesus Christ was assisting him, they said to one another: “Where did this priest come from, for we have not seen him except today? How beautiful is his priestly service and his ‘race’ is not like someone wearing flesh but like the Seraphim!”.
ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎሙ ፡ አተዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡፣ ወዜነዉ" ለሰብኦሙ ። ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ ጵ ፡ እንግዳ ፡ ቀሲስ ፣ ዘቀደሰ ፡ ዮም ፡ ምንተ ፣ ይሜኒ ፡ ጥንቁቅ” ፡ መልእክቱ፡' ፡ ወቃሉኒ ፡ ያስተፌሥሕ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ስታየ፡ ፡ ወይን ። ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አይቴኑ” ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወይቤልዎሙ ፡

go?” and they told them: “As to where he went we don’t know. When we told him: ‘Stay with us overnight!’, he told us: ‘As for today, leave me and tomorrow | will return’. We told him: ‘And today where will you spend the night?’. And he told us: ‘Where God has wished”. Having heard this, those who listened said to one another: “If God allows it, tomorrow we will go to the church and will receive the Eucharist by the hands of that priest”. Saying this, they spent that night and on the next day they went to the church. Abuna Yonas presides the Eucharist in Jerusalem again: someone recognizes him 41 When it was the time of the Eucharistic celebration, our father Yonas arrived again and wore the vestments of the Eucharistic celebration. Those people who had seen him earlier told them: “This is the one about whom we spoke to you, yesterday he celebrated the Eucharist here”. At that time there were those who knew our father Yonas in the country of Ethiopia. They were dismayed and they told them: “This is indeed our father Yonas! When did he come here?”. They thought that he came in the flesh. Thence, after he completed the Eucharistic celebration, he distributed the Holy Communion to them and after this he disappeared from them and from this they knew that he had come in the Spirit. And from among those people there are those who went out to the country of Ethiopia and reported all that happened. Also this is a wonderful thing, astounding to the one hearing it. May the blessing of his prayer be with us. Amen.

### A disciple tries to set alight the monastery

42 And again we tell and proclaim the meekness, the humility of our father Yonas and the might that he did. There was one man among the disciples of our father Damyanos who was seized by the trap of Satan. He went out from the community of the saints and lived with the mockers. One day among days he visited the monastery and they welcomed him with great joy. They counselled him and told him: “Remember the word of the Books and do not walk in treachery, for, this world promises sweet (things) but pays back bitter (things)’. So they told him, they welcomed him with a good welcome and he ate and drank. He took leave from them that very evening because he had guile in his heart. When the silence of the night came he took coal and put it in the house but he did not succeed in the burning though he strove to burn it.
ይትጋደል ፡ ለአንድዶታ" ። ወእንዘ ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ርእዮ ፣ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐ ውር ፡ ኀበ ፡ለአክዎ ፡እስመ ፡ ጽማዌ ፡ሌሊት ፡ውእቱ ፡ ጊዜሁ ። ወከልሐ ፣

saying: “Father so-and-so has destroyed the inhabited place by fire”. The inhabitants came out and ran and arrived at that house but they did not find him because he had run away and escaped from them. When they lifted their eyes, they saw the coal in the middle of the grass but it was not burning. They brought out the coal; they overturned the grass but did not find any burning at all. On account of this we ought to say: “Very great is your work oh Lord (Ps 91:58) and wonderful is your name”. And again we say: “Awesome is God over his Holy ones. To him be glory forever. Amen”.

44 The following day they searched for that man in the places of the town and found him in a house of a governor and they told that governor their case. He replied and said to them: “If it were for others | would have offered myself on his behalf. But what shall | do with the word of our father Yonas, for | trust him with the fullness of my heart and | make him like my God”. And he told them: “Take him and deliver him and let them justify him according to his will’. Tying him he gave (him) to the envoys and told them: “I will also come tomorrow and will join you in court, take him and deliver him to our father Yonas!”. And he spoke to him in meekness without anger and told him: “Oh so-and-so, how did Satan fill your heart (Acts 5:3) and subjugated you so that you (could) burn by fire the house of your father? Did you not know what the people of the world mean by that parable: ‘the father's house is the returning place of the children!’. And now repent and ask for forgiveness that he may pardon you” (Acts 8:22). Trial of the arsonist at the presence of the governor: Abuna Yonas forgives him
ወበሳኒታሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፣ ዕለት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ሥዩም ፣ ወአስተኳ ነኖሙ ። ወተሞአ” ፣ ብፍትሕ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ"' ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ምንተ ፡ ትብል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መዐምፅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተሞአ”" ፡ በፍ ትሕ” ፡ሞትኒ ፡ ወሕይወትኒ”' ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ፈቀድከ ፡ እስሮ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ፈቀድከ ፡ቅሥፎ ። ወአውሥአ” ፡ረባን ፡ በቃል ፡ ጥዑም ፡ወይቤ ፡

አንሰ ፣ ኢየአስሮ' ፡ ወኢይቀሥፎ' ፣ ይስረይ' ፣ ሎቱ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወይ ሚጦ ፡ውስተ ፡ ንስሐ ፡ እስመ ፡እተቱየ ፡ውእቱ ፡ወአሐዝን' ፡በተሐጐሎቱ ። ወእግዚእነሂ፤ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተስእሎ ፡ አቡነ ፣ ጴጥሮስ ፡፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ አበሰ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እኀየ ፡ እስከ' ፡፣ ስብዕኑ' ፡ እኅድግ፤ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግ ዚእነ ፡ ኢይብለከ' ፡ ስብዐ'' ፡ አላ ፡ ሰብዓ'' ፡ በበስብዕ'' ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ሥዩም ፡ አንከረ'' ፡፣ የውሀቶ ፣ ወተሰናዓሎ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ፍኖቶ ።
neither jail nor whip him, may God pardon him and turn him into repentance for he is my brother and | grieve for his cunning”. Our Lord too, when our father Peter asked him, and told him: If my brother sins against me, shall | forgive him until seven (times)?, our Lord told him: | don’t tell you seven but seventy times seven (Mt 18:21-22). Hearing this that governor admired his meekness, took leave from him and went his way.

### The arsonist goes to Dabra Maryam to accuse Abuna Yonas
ወውእቱኒ ፣ መነኮስ ፣ አስረፃ"' ፡ ካዕበ'' ፣ ለእኪት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፣ ወሖረ ፡፣ ኀበ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ደብር" ፡ እንተ ፡ ይብልዋ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይነብር ፣ መምህረ ፣ መምህራን ፣ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡፣ ክርስቶስ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝኩ ፣ መዓምፅ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ገብረ'' ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አእመርከኑ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ አቡነ" ፣ ዮናስ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ገብረ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ መዐምፅ ፡ ለዘከማየ ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ አሰሮ” ፣ እደዊሁ ፡፣ ወእገሪሁ ፣ በመዋቅሕት ፡ ወወደዮ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ግብ ፡ ተመሲሎ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘኢለብሰ'' ፡ ሥጋ ። ወሞተ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እሱር" ። ወኢአምኖ” ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለጠቢብ ፡ ኩሉ” ፣
46 But that monk made his heart germinate evil and he went to a monastery that they call Dabra Maryam where our father Gabra Krastos, the master of the masters lived. That wicked told our father Gabra Krastos: “Did you know what our father Yonas has done?”. And he told him: “What did he do?”. That wicked told him: “He tied with chains the hands and the feet of a sinner like me and put him in a pit, (treating him) as if he did not wear flesh. That prisoner died inside it’. But our father Gabra Krestos did not believe him “because the wise realizes everything”.

### Gabra Krestos the abbot of Dabra Maryam consults Abuna Yonas
ይትዐወቆ ። ወልኢከሰ” ፣ ለአከ" ፣ ሎቱ" ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አማንኑ ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ኮነ ፣ በኀቤከ ። ወበጽሑ ፡ ላእካን” ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮኖስ ፡ ፣ ካህናት ፣ ወዕለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፡ በቀዳሚት ፡ ሰሙነ ፡ አጽዋም ፡ ወሎቱሰ ፡ ኢረከብዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ለጸዊም” ፡ በከመ ፡ ያለምድ ። ወለአኩ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አርድእቲሁ” ፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ሳእካን"'' ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ" ፡ ይቤለከአ ፡ አባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ብጻሕአ ፣ ኀቤየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እስአልከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ሉከ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ገበርከ” ፡ ወሊተኒ” ፡ ኢያአምነኒ” ፡ ነገሩ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናኖስ ፡ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ መልእክት ፡ አክሞሰሰ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዝሰ ፡ ነገር ፡ እምሰይጣን ፡ውእቱ ፡ከመ ፡ያውዕፅአኒ ፡እምበአትየ”" ። ወከመ ፡ ይትባህነኒ" ፡ ምዝጋናየ" ፣
47 He sent indeed a message to him saying: “Is it true that such a thing has taken place in you?”. The envoys, four priests, reached our father Yonas, on the Friday, in the first week of the Fasts but they did not find him because he had gone out to the desert to fast as was his custom. His disciples sent a message to him as the ministers had said, saying: “Father Gabra Kreastos is saying to you: ‘Come to me that | may ask you, because they are saying that you did such a thing, but the matter does not convince me”. When our father Yonas heard this word of the message, he smiled and said: “As for this thing, it is from Satan in order to draw me out from my cave, and that my reward may become void (1 Cor 9:15)”.

you!’ The Book of Psalms says: There is a time for God to act (Ps 118:126). In another (place) too it says: There is time for every work (Eccl 3:1). Paul also said: For me it is better to die on account of God than to be deprived of my reward (1 Cor 9:15) and tell my father: ‘You know my mind and it is not possible for me to come to you. | will come to you after finishing the work that | have started’. They told the ministers as he had told them.

### Reactions of the envoys of Gabra Krestos
ወአዕበዩ ፡ ጸሪፈ" ፣ ላዕለ ፡ አቡነ" ፣ ፻፤' ፡ ላእካን ፡ ወነበቡ ፡ በከመ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ወራብዖሙሰ ፡ ለእሉ ፣ ተምዖሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትነቡ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ለብእሴ" ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ደብርኒ” ፣ ተየውሁ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነድድ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽርፈቶሙ ። እስመ ፡ መሀሮሙ ፡ እገዘ ፣ ይብል” ፣ ኢትፍድዩ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ህየንተ ፣ እኩይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፍዳሰ፡' ፣ ዘእግዚአብ ሔር” ። ወተሰናዓልዎሙ ፡ በእኩይ ፡ ወአስተፋነውዎሙ ፡ በሠናይ ፣ ሰብአ ፡ ደብር ። ወምስሌሆሙ ፣ ዝኩ ፡፣ መስተዋድይ ፡ ለገቢረ ፣ ሕሱም ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትሬሰይ ። ወሖሩ ፣ ፍኖቶሙ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘፈነዎሙ ፡ ወነገሩ ፡፣ መል እክቶሙ ።
49 Three of the envoys insulted all the more our father and they spoke as they wished but the fourth among them was angry with them and told them: “Why do you speak ill against the man of God?”. And the people of the monastery were gentle with them even though their heart was burning because of their insult. For he had taught them, saying: “Do not pay back evil for evil (Rom 12:17a) for retribution belongs to God” (Deut 32:35). Those departed from them badly but the people of the monastery bade them a good farewell. That calumniator was with them, getting ready to do evil. They went their way and reached the one who sent them and conveyed their message.

### Fire descends upon the envoys that insulted Abuna Yonas
ወወረደ ፡፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ እሳተ ፡ መዓቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ዮኮ" ፡ ወለዝኩሰ”፥ ፣ መስተዋድይ ፡ ተአስረ ፡ ልሳኑ ። ዘንተ ፡ መከራ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ እሉ ፣ 8" ፣፡ ወ፻ ፡ እምኔሆሙ” ፣ ሞቱ ፡ ወ፳ ፡ ብህመ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ነቢበ ፡ ወኀምሶሙሰ”' ፡ ቴር” ፣ ዘተረክበ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተረፈ ፡፣ መዋዕል ፡ ጸግበ ፣ ሞተ ። እሉሰ” ፡ እለ ፡ ተቀሥፉ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ትብጻሕ ፣ ኀምሲት" ፡ ሰንበተ ፡ ጾም ።
50 The fire of God’s anger descended upon those three and the tongue of the calumniator was bound. Those four met this ordeal, three of them died and one became so dumb that he could not speak but the fifth of them, the one found meek, died after being satiated with abundant days. As for the ones that were stricken down, (it happened) before the fifth Sabbath of Lent arrived.

### Abuna Yonas pays a visit to Gabra Krestos
ወሐሊፎ ፡ በዐለ ፡ ፋሲካ ፡ ሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካነ ፡ አቡነ ፣ ገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወተቀበሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ተቀብ ሎተ ፡ በፍሥሕ ፡ ልብ ፡ ወበብሩህ ፡ ኅሊና'' ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሐዌሳ" ፡ ለምጽአ ትከ" ፡ ኦዳግማዊ” ፣ ኤልያስ ፡ ወፅአት ፡ እሳተ ፡ መዐት ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፣ ወበልዐቶሙ ፣ ለ፻። ፣ እለ ፡ አሕሰሙ ፡፣ ኃለ ፡ ላዕሌከ ። ወፅ፳ ፣ ሀለወ ፡ ፣ወኢሀለወ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለወ" ። ወአሐዱ'' ፡ ዝኢያሕሰመ ፡ ወኢተ ዐደወ" ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ሐይወ ፡፣ ወመንክራቲከ ፡ ዜነወ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ተአምኖ ፡ አምኃ ፡ መንፈሳዊት” ፡ ወተዛነዉ"' ፡ ዳህና ፡ ብሔር ፡
51 After the feast of Easter passed our father Yonas went to the place of our father Gabra Krastos, and our father Gabra Krestos welcomed him with a good welcome with a joyful heart and shining mind. And told him: “Welcome to your coming, oh second Elijah! A fire of anger has come out from God and devoured those three who spoke evil against you. And one exists even though he did not exist while he was existing’ (Rev 17:8), but the one who did not do evil and transgress against you, life he lived and proclaimed your wonders”. Having said this he greeted him with a spiritual greeting and they recounted to one another the safety of the country (Ps 121:6).

### Abuna Yonas returns home and pardons the arsonist

52 They spent the night together and the next day they bade farewell and he went his way and returned to his home but as far as that calumniator is concerned, a great sickness met him, his throat was shut and his tongue was tied, he beseeched and asked for the pardon of his sin and of his guilt. And this glorious father, our father Yonas was interceding on his behalf evening and morning and was shedding tears from his eyes. And when the day of his call arrived, then he said: “God pardoned me by the prayer of our father Yonas’”. May he apportion to us an inheritance with him, our father Yonas, amen.

### Abunéa Yonas the water diviner
ካዕበ' ፣ ስምዑ ፣ ንንግርክሙ ፣ ትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ሣህል ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ተረፈ ፡ ነቢያት ። ሱታፌ ፡ ጻማሆሙ” ፣ ለሰማዕት ። ጸዋሬ ፡ ሥርዐቶሙ ፡ለመነኮሳት ። ወኮነ ፡በ፳ዘመን ፡ ሕጸተ"' ፣ ማይ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወብዙኀ ፡ ገዓሩ ፡ መነኮሳት" ፡ ኅዳጥ" ፣ ማይ” ፣ ቦሙ ፡ "ወአኮ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ዓለምኒ” ኅዳጥ" ፣ ቦሙ” ። ወለመበለታትሰ ፣ የብሰ ፡ ማየ ፣ ዓዘቃቲሆን ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሆን ፡ ፀዕር” ፣ ዘጽምእ'' ። ሰብአ”' ፡ ዓለምኒ ፡ ኢይሁብዎን" ፡ ማየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ በሜጥ ። ወመነኮሳትሰ” ፣ ኢያቀርብዎን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሰኬዛሆሙ" ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ሥር ዓት ፣ ቦሙ" ።
53 Hearken ye again, so that we tell you the virtues of the blessed father, the work that our father Yonas, the follower of the prophets, performed in Dabra Sahl. He is the sharer of the martyrs’ toill Bearer of the regulations of the monks! Once upon a time there was shortage of water among the people of a town; the monks cried a lot (because) they had little water and not a lot, and the people of the world too had a little. The water of the widows’ wells dried up and the pain of thirst descended upon them and the people of the world would not give them water without payment. And the monks did not allow them to draw near their dams, because they had a rule (on this matter).


[p. 354] {#po.53.p354}

### Abuna Yonas revives dry dams
ወአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እመዋዕል ፡ ኃጥአ ፡ ማየ ፡ እም ፡ ሰኬዛ ። ወአ ውየዋ ፡ ወገዐራ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ገዐረ ። ወዜነውዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘረከ ቦን ፡ ወጸሎተ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ጸዊሮ ፡ መጽሐፈ" ፡ ገድሉ ፣ ለአቡነ ፡ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ ። ወጸቢሖ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ጸውዐ ፡ እለ ፡ ኀረየ” ፡ እምአርድ እቲሁ"“ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ያድኅንክሙ፤! ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድሉ" ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ኤዎስ ጣቴዎስ ፡ ወአንብቡ" ፣ ኀበ ፡፣ ሰኬዛ ፡ ዘመበለታት ፣ ወወሀቦሙ ፣ ትእምርተ" ፣ ኀበ ፡ዘያነቡ" ፣ ወአንበቡ ። ወአንቢቦሙ ፡ ሰገዱ ፡ በበ ፡ ጓ ፡ እስመ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ አዘዞሙ ። ወከሪዮሙ ፡ ዐዘቅተ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ አውፁኡ"' ፡ ኅዳጠ ፡ መሬተ” ፡
54 One day among days water was missing from four dams. They howled and cried out loudly. They recounted to our father Yonas all that had happened to them and on that night he prayed a prayer carrying the book of the gad of our father Ewostatewos. When it was morning he called the ones he had chosen (Mk 3:13) from among his disciples and told them: “May the book of the gadl of our father of Ewostatewos save youl”. And they read at the dam of the widows and he indicated to them the spot in which they would read, and they read. After reading they made forty prostrations because so he had ordered them. While they were digging a cistern, after they had


[p. 356] {#po.53.p356}
ወፅአ" ፡ ማይ"፤ ፡ ወመልአ ፡ አፈ ፡ ዐዘቅት" ። ወኢያብሖሙ ፡ 'ይክርዩ ፡ ዳግመ" ፣ ወእምአሜሃ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ" ፡ ሀለወት ፣ ምልእታ። ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዐዘቅት ።
extracted a little earth, water came out and filled the brim of the cistern. And (the swell of the water) did not allow them to dig again and since then until now that cistern is full.

### Abunda Yonas restores water to more dead dams

55 And again, after this fact, our father Yonas went out to teach and arrived in one of the villages of Sarawe. In that village a great thirst took place because water was exhausted from their cisterns. The people of the village told him: “Father! Do for us what you did in your village because both we humans and our animals are perishing for the lack of water! Bless the dam of our country and extract water for us!”. He blessed for them a desolate place that did not have a source of water before then. He told them: “Dig here and God will accomplish for you!”. They dug and water came out and it filled up to the brim the cistern that they dug. And that (well) is here until today quenching (the thirst) of humans and of animals. They were full of admiration and were astounded, and because of this they named this blessed one, Mata‘s the Second, because of his extracting of water to quench the thirst of many. Abuna Yonas sends two disciples to bishop Gabr'el for altar consecration 56 And again let me tell that he was a seer of hidden things and there was no deed that was concealed from him. And one day among days, this blessed one wanted to send (a message) to the bishop so that he would consecrate an altar. He told two of his disciples: “Go to the bishop, father Gabr'el and bring (the tabot) so that he consecrates it for us and make haste to get it to Dabra Qwesqwam on the (feast) of the entry of our Lady (to the Temple)”, because in those days there was no consecration of altars, except on that specific day. And they did accordingly and they got there on the month of Hedar 5; they gave that altar to the bishop that he would consecrate it for
ሎሙ ፡ ወቀደሰ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወአግብኡ ፡ ጸዊሮሙ ፡ ታቦቶሙ ፡ ወአተዉ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ዕፁብ ፡ ውእቱ ። በረከቱ፤ ፡ ትኩን፤ ፡-ምስሌነ' ፡ አሜን ።
them and he consecrated it for them. They brought back their altar carrying it and returned to their country; this too is wonderful! May his blessing be with us, amen.

### King Zar'a Ya‘aqob calls a meeting to discuss the Sabbath

57 And again hearken ye, what our father Yonas, the follower of the teaching of Peter and Paul did, wearer of the askema like Anthony and Macarius, legislator of community (life) like Pachomius! And while he was in the area of Bur and was teaching until the length of Snba Sannayt and the time of Lent arrived. He returned to the area of Bur and reached a monastery whose name was Barakenaha, the graveyard of the 150 Righteous, for it was his monastery. He shut himself there in his cave and there was nobody who visited him or saw his face except two of his disciples. And before that year King Zar'a Ya‘aqob had summoned the disciples of Ma‘aqaba Agzi’s for a regulation agreement with the bishop, that the bishop honours the Sabbath and the disciples of Ma‘eqaba Agzi’s receive priesthood (from the bishop) because they were rejecting one another. Abuna Yonas accepts the King’s invitation but is worried about presents for the monarch


[p. 358] {#po.53.p358}

58 Envoys from nebura ed Nob came to our father Yonas and told him: “Don't be bored to go to the king’s court and do not be separated from your brothers for a king has reigned who agrees with your ordinance!”. Having heard this, he gathered together the monks of the monastery and told them: “As for going, it is better that we go, but there is one thing that frightens me. If the king says: ‘Receive goods’, how are we going to take them for free? And how do we refuse while our brothers accept, for mighty is the word of the king. But let us do one thing: | heard that in the town so-and-so there are ostrich birds, let us buy and give those as a blessing to the king so that we will not take for free the king’s goods”. He told them: “Convey to πϑρυγᾶ ad Nob and do what he tells you”.

59 They conveyed as he told them to do and he also told them: “Give (it) to father Gabra Krestos” for he is the master of the masters and he enjoys benevolence before the king. They brought (it) to father Gabra Krastos and he gave them a gift for the king and they spent the rainy season there. The disciples of Ma‘eqaba Agzi’e agreed with the bishop in matters of ecclesiastic legislation, that they receive the priesthood and that the bishop honours the Sabbath.

### A detail on the aftermath of the Council summoned by the king

60 One day while he was in his cave and a disciple was at the door of his cave for there is a perennial regulation that he (i.e. Yonas) listens to him whenever he knocked softly so that he (i.e. the disciple) might visit him and do what he told him. One day a companion of that disciple told him: “Good news for you my brother! Our men have returned and the king has given them plenty of goods: forty mules, forty marway garments, forty purple garments”. As the companion was telling him this, he (i.e. Yonas) was listening in to him while he was in his cave praying. When it was the sixth hour of the day, our father Yonas came to the place where that disciple who wrote was, because he loved him and told him: “Do you say it is true what this companion of yours has told you about the riches of goods (received)?”. That disciple replied and told him: Ἵ am also asking you, my father. Do you say that it is (too) much or do you say it is (too) little?”. The holy father replied and told him: “I will tell you with certainty: leave the mules aside and do not accept them, as for the purple garment, they have given them five garments and five marway garments too, there is nothing that exceeds this. Keep my word until you find it in its time”. When those envoys returned, it happened like thus, the word of this chosen father did not diminish nor increase. That disciple said: “| saw with my eyes what | heard with my ear from the mouth of my master!”. May his blessing be with us, amen.

### Abuna Yonas takes care of a disciple craving priesthood
ወካዕበ ፡ እዜኑ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትመስላ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በይእቲ ፣ ጾማዕት ፡ ወውእቱኒ' ፡ ረድእ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወበይእቲ' ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽኦ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ተቃሕዎ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እለ' ፡ ተሠይሙ ፣ ክህነተ፤ ፡ መሐዛ ኒሁ፤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ አብሐኒ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንግርከ ፡ ትካዝየ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አባሕኩከ ፡ ንብብ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ እሖር' ፡ ኅበ ፣ ጳጳስ ፡ እኩን ፡ ካህነ ፡ ከመ ፣ አብያጽየ' ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፈቲወሰ' ፣፡ የ‹አነኒ ፡፣ እምፈተውኩ" ፣ እርአይከ ፡፣ ቀሲሰ'"' ፣ ከዊነከ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ተዐገሥ ፡ እስከ ፣ እነግሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጾማዕቱ ፡ ወተማሕለለ' ፡ መጠነ ፡ ጓ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይጥዕም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ግማሰ ፣ ኅብስት ፡ ወይእቲኒ"'' ፣ በሣልሲት"' ፡ ዕለት ፣ እስመ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ በሐምሌ ፡
61 | narrate again something similar to this one while he was in the same cave and the same disciple was with him. In those days, rivalry with his peers who were appointed priest came to that disciple. He said to our father Yonas: “Allow me, my father, to tell you my need!” and he told him: “I have permitted you. Speak!”. He told him: “I want to go to the bishop that | may become priest like my companions”. Our father told him: “As for wishing, | would also wish to see you become a priest but only be patient until | tell God”. He entered his cave and interceded for the length of forty days without tasting except half a bread and even that on the third day because that was his custom during Hamle and Nahase and on Pagwemen.

62 When it was the feast of the Assumption of our Lady Mary, while people were at the morning service, he called that disciple and told him: “As for what you said, it has not happened for its time has not yet arrived”. And that disciple too said: “Let the will of God be done! Is the house of my father destroyed for lack of priests?”. The holy father told him: “Yes! As for today be patient and when God allows you will go”. He told him this because he knew that a great illness would meet him.

### The disciple longing for priesthood falls sick

63 And again that disciple told him: “Father | go to my mother”. And the father, the seer of hidden things told him: “Go then and come back on such a date and do not delay”. He told him this knowing in the Spirit what would happen to him. He went to his mother and returned (to the monastery) on that very day. The holy one told him: “Welcome on your return, my son, who did not transgress my command”. He blessed him and during that night he slept. When he woke up from the sleep he found all his body parts battered and could not move; it was as if he was beaten by sticks. And our father knew what had happened to him and sent (a message) to him saying: “So-and-so are you fine? What happened to you that you did not come to church, even though you are the keeper of its services?”. That disciple said: “Father forgive me, for it is not due to my laziness but when | woke up from my sleep | found all my body parts battered and could not move even to pour the water of the law of the flesh”. Our father replied to him and told him: “My son,

64 That disciple lived for seventeen months and when these months were completed, health came upon him. Then our father told him: “So-and-so, from now on get ready to go to the bishop to become priest presbyter, because the time has arrived and take with you Ma‘eqaba Agzi's, so that he may become a deacon”. He told him: “Oh my father how can | now go, for my back and my knees are not firm, but at your word | say, ‘yes’. Our father told him: “Do not be afraid my son! From now on, the time of illness has passed. Even before, | told you: ‘Be patient’, knowing this, that you would not fall sick in an alien country. Now though be steadfast!”. And he received his blessing and was consecrated priest and that disciple was consecrated deacon, and after they were consecrated they returned to their country. Look you, how thus wonderfully and astonishingly he knows beforehand what would take place afterwards. The wonders of his visions, if they were reported accurately in writing, they would have no end (Jn 21:25). May his blessing be upon us.

65 Again, one day among days, while he was in Sarawe, while he was passing to teach, our father Yonas, of hammering tongue and milky lips, arrived at a town together with his disciples, a great rain fell upon them and they wanted to enter the town and people of the town welcomed them as those that speak well, but they made them swear by God, the Lord of the heavens and of the earth (Pr Man 1:2) that they would not enter their town. The holy father feared (not to break) that oath and they directed their going to a town near to it. He left those mean fellows with the avarice in their heart. He took his disciples and went his way where he wanted (to go) and came to a town in front of him. They welcomed them with a good welcome and returned home.

People that refused hospitality to Abuna Yonas punished with fire from heaven 66 His disciples that were with him, told him: “Father! Show your might over that town that did not welcome you, otherwise we have sworn by the life of your soul that we will not say that you are our father”. Our father Yonas told them: “Keep silent, my children! What is your worry? For God himself knows what he is going to do”. After this matter, before supper was over, a fire came out and destroyed the houses of the mean fellows. There was nothing from their houses that (the fire) spared: neither food nor butter, nor honey, nor sack, nor saddlebag, nor rope, nor pack animal, nor thong, nor other utensils in their house.

### Abuna Yonas pardons the unwelcoming people
ወአቡነሰ ፡ አእመሮ” ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ወሐዘነ" ፣ ዐቢየ ፡ ሐዘነ'' ፣ ወተዘኪሮ” ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ እግዚእነ" ፣ አፍቅሩ ፡ ጸላእተክሙ” ፡ ወደኃርዎሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይረግሙክሙ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ በእን ቲአሆሙ ፣ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ። ወበሳኒታሃ” ፣ ለይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አሌለዩ" ፡ ገዬሰ፡' ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ውዕየ” ፣ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ነሚኦሙ ፡ ሕራራተ” ፣ እክሎሙ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አባ ፡ ሥረይ" ፡ ለነ ፡ እስመ ፡ በእበድነ ፡ ዘረከበነ'' ፡ ዝንቱ" ፡ ወበርቱዕ ፡ ይደልወነ ፣ ወተፈደይነ ፣ በከመ ፡ ምግባሪነ ። ወሰአልዎ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይባርኮሙ ፡ ወባረኮሙ ፣ ወነሚኦሙ" ፣ በረከቶ ፡ አተዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብ ያቲሆሙ ። ወወፅአ” ፣ ስሙዐተ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ" ፡ ሀገር ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ትኩን” ፡ ምስሌነ'' ፡ አሜን” ።
67 Our father knew indeed in the Spirit that this had taken place and was greatly afflicted and remembering what our Lord had said: Love your enemies and bless those who curse you (Mt 5:44). For this reason he prayed his God for forgiveness on their behalf. On the day following that night, those people whose town was burnt, rose by night to go taking their burnt food. They told him: “Father forgive us, for this has met us because of our foolishness, we have rightly deserved it, and we have been repaid according to our deeds”. And they asked him to bless them. He blessed them and they returned to their houses after receiving his blessing. His fame went out to the whole territory, may his prayer and blessing be with us, amen.

Abunda Yonas
ወካዕበ” ፣ ስምዑ ፡ ንንግርክሙ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ወአኀዊነ ፡ ዘነገሩነ ፡ እለ ፣ ሰምዑ ፡ ወርእዩ ፡፣ ዘገብረ ፡ መንክረ ። ወእንዘ ፣ ሀለወ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ አቡነ ፣ ዮኖስ ፣ በደብሩ” ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለአከት ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ፣ብእሲቱ ፣ ለመኩንነ" ፣ ሠራዌ። ፣ ዝስሙ ፡ ብሌን” ፡ አስገደ“ ፡ ወብእሲቱ" ፡ ስማ ፡ መግደላዊት ። ወክልኤሆሙ" ፣ ሠናያን ፡ ወመፍቀርያነ ፡ መነኮሳት ። ወለአከት ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ አባ ፡ አባ ፡ ኢትርሣዕ ፡ ኪዳነከ”" ፡ ተማኅፀነ ፡ በጸሎትከ” ። አኮኑ ፣ ንሕነ ፡ የምጥዋን ፡ ለከ"! ፣ ነፍስነ ፡ ወሥጋነ ፡ አነ ፡ ወእግዚአየ ፣
68 Again hearken you, so that we may narrate to you, our fathers and brothers, the wonder he did and that those who listened and saw told us. While our blessed father Yonas was in his holy monastery, the wife of the governor of Sarawe, whose name was Belen Asagada, sent a dispatch to him. The name of his wife was Magsdalawit. Both of them were good and lovers of the monks. She sent a message to him saying: “Father! Father! Do not forget your pact, we have confided in your prayer. Are we not given to you

ዘረሰዩ ፡ ሊተ ፡ መፃምርትየ' ። በጽሐኑ' ፡ አርምሞ ፡ ወተጸምሞ'" ፡ እንዘ' ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ዴደ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአነኒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፣ በብሔረ" ፡ ባዕድ ። ወኢናሐዝነካኑ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንሕነሰ' ፡ ረሰይኖከ ፡ አበ ፡ ወእመ ። ጸሊ ፣ ለነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርአየነ ፡ ዐይነ' ፡ ምሕረቱ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢትርስዓሃ ፡ አንብረነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕልቀት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብከ ። ወከመ ፡ ማዕተብ ፡ በመዝራ ዕትከ'' ።
in our soul and in our body, me and my lord whom they made a spouse for me? Has the silence and deafness while my lord was at the king’s court and while | was in a foreign country reached? Do we not move you to compassion towards us, for we have made you (our) father and mother? Pray for us that the eye of God’s mercy may see us. Do not forget us: put us as a ring in your heart and as a seal on your arm (Song 8:6)”.

### Abuna Yonas sends a disciple to console her

69 Having heard the word of his daughter's envoys, the bowels of his belly were stirred, because she was a listener of his word and doer of his will. His heart was moved to goodness and he called his favourite disciple and told him: “Did you hear what Magedalawit said? She said thus and thus. And now, take with you some food from the nourishment of the community and tell her: ‘You will see God’s power, his mercy and his forgiveness before this food is finished’”. That disciple went and told her as his master had told him, and then she said: “I trust the prayer of my father Yonas for everything will be accomplished by his prayer’. Before that food was finished her husband returned from the king’s court having been appointed with a greater authority over Sdrawe. She returned to the king's house with the rest of that food. She glorified God saying: “Glory to God who did not neglect the petition of his servants and disclosed his mystery to his loved ones” (1 Cor 2:10). This one that you have heard too is wonderful: that what happened in her favour was what he told her. May the blessing of his prayer be with us, amen. Dispute between Abuna Yonas’s monks and a farmer working under them

70 And again, hearken you, so that we may tell you the virtues that our father Yonas, the pillar of light, performed in Dabra Dahuhan, and of the good monks within it, who were in agreement and united (among themselves), and lovers of guests, obedient and humble, equal in conduct and equal in lifestyle, in food and in every utensil. As they were living in this way, they had a dispute with a lay person who was their employee, on account of fields. They told him:

“As for that one do not plough it because it is near our fields!”. Having said this they made him swear a great oath, but he broke (the oath) and ploughed The heart of those holy (monks) was saddened and they slaughtered his ox The dispute takes a sad turn: it engulfs Abuna Yonas and his own monks 71 Our father Yonas though was not in that place and when he heard this he was sad and came to them and told them: “Why did you do so?”. They told him: “Because he has offended the name of the Lord his God!”. They had a meek steward; he too was not with them. He also told them: “Why did you do 507". Those monks told our father Yonas and that steward: “If you quarrel with us to favour laymen, we will leave your monastery to you and we will go where we want. And also you steward, know that if we stay here you will not be our steward”. Our father Yonas told them: “Is it not enough for you what you did before?”. Those monks told our father Yonas: “We will leave for you your monastery and we will go where we want because none of those to whom we drew near hates us”. As the blessed father saw that they were enraged, he undertook to speak to them in meekness, for so was his custom. He began to pray to God and said: “Lord! Lord! Let not them fall into the error of their heart! Rebuke them with the stick of the children (Deut 8:5; Heb 12:6) and not with the punishment of a stranger’.

### God punishes with death the protesting monks
ሠፍት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአት ፡ እደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጽንዕት ፡ ወቀሠፈቶሙ ፣ ወኢያምሠጡ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኅዳጣን ። በዝኒ” ፡ ኢያእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፣ ለአቡነ ፣ ዮኖስ ፡ ረከበቶሙ ፡ ዛቲ” ፡ መቅሠፍት ። ወካዕበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮኖስ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እመሰ ፡፣ እስከ ፣ ይእዜ ፡ ኢያእመሩ ፡ ወመዓቶሙኒ ፡ ኢያቀ፦ ረሩ ፡ አንተ ፣፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ግበር ፡ በዘ ፡ የአምሩ" ፡ ወበዘ ፡ ይገርሩ” ። ወሶበ ፣ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጸለለ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ጽላሎተ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወአኀዙ ፣ ይሙቱ ።
72 Thereafter the firm hand of God rose and punished them and they did not escape except a few (of them). In this also they did not know that it was by the prayer of our father Yonas that that punishment met them. Our father Yonas prayed again and said: “If they have not known so far and have not made their anger to cool down, you Lord, act in such a way that they may know and be subdued”. As he said this, the shadow of death, hovered upon them and they started to die.

### Some of the protesters are spared after they beg Abuna Yonas’s pardon

73 At that time they said to one another: “What happened to this our father Yonas that he does not pray for our tribulation?”. There was a disciple who was serving our father Yonas. They called him and told him: “Go, tell our father: ‘So are your disciples saying — Come and let us humble ourselves, carrying stones, for it is because we have saddened him that we have got this punishment”. That disciple went and told that to our father Yonas. Having heard this, our father Yonas said: “If God has made them wise and knowing,

ወሖረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ለአኃው ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይበሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወእሙንቱኒ' ፣ ሖሩ፤ ፣ እብነ' ፡ ጸዊሮሙ፤ ፡ ወበከዩ" ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ አባ ፡ አባ ፣ አበስነ' ፣ እስመ ፡ መሰልከነ ፡ ሰብአ' ፡ ከማነ" ፡ "ወይእዜኒ ፡ መሐረነ ፡ ከመ ፣ አምላክከ ። አባ ፡ አባ ፣ አስተምሕር ፡ ለነ' ፣ እስመ' ፣ አበስነ ፡ ወጌገይነ ፣ በቅድሜከ ። ይፀራዕ፤' ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንቤ'' ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ዘአንተ ፡ ትቤ ። ወበጊዜሀ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ወተሰምዐ'' ፡ "ጳሎቱ ፡ ኀበ" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ እስመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይፈርህዎ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ቅሩብ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለየዋሀነ ፡ ልብ ። አስምዐ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ወአድኀነ ፡ መርዔቶ ። ወኀደገ ፣ መዐተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ እምላዕለ ፣ ደቂቁ ፡ እለ ፡ ደብረ" ፡፣ ድኃን ። እስመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ድኀኃን ፡ ብሂል ፡ ደብረ ፡ጻድቃን ፡ ብሂል ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ መዘምር ፡ አድባረ ፣፡ ድጉ ኃነ ፡ ይሰመያ'!' ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ። ወጸቢሖ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ይቤ ሎሙ" ፣ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ” ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ኦደቂቅየ” ፡ ሶበሰ ፡ ኢተጋነይክሙ ፡ ትማልም ፣ ይመስለኒ ፡ እምኢተረፈ" ፡ ፳ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ዛቲ ፡ ደብር ። ወይእ ዜኒ ፣ ሰረየ" ፡ ለክሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይእዜሰ” ፣ ኢትድግሙ ፡ ዘከ መዝ ፡ ግብረ” ። ወይቤልዎ ፣ አሆ” ፡ አባ ፣ ዛቲ ፡ ተግሣጽ ፣ ትኩነነ” ፡ እም ዮም ፡ እስከ"' ፡ ለዓለም ። ወዘንተ ፡ ገብረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረ
let them act accordingly!”. That disciple went and spoke to the brothers as our father told him and they went indeed carrying a stone and wept and said: “Father! Father! We have sinned because we thought you were a human like us but now forgive us like your God. Father! Father! Intercede for us for we have sinned and erred before you. Let what we said be set aside and let what you have said be done!”. 74 He prayed immediately and his prayer was heard before God, because it says: God is near those who fear him (Ps 84:10) and again it says: “God is near the meek of heart” (Ps 33:19). He made his prayer heard and saved his flock. God’s anger with his disciples from Dabra Dehuhan ceased by the prayer of our father Yonas. For Dabra Dehuhan means Monastery of the Righteous. As the Singer says: “The souls of the righteous will be called ‘excavated rocks”. Early in the morning, our father Yonas told his disciples: “My children, if yesterday you had not humbled yourselves, | think that not one would have survived in this monastery. But now God has forgiven you. And now, do not repeat such an action”. They told him: “Let it be, father! May this punishment serve us from now until eternity”. Our father Yonas did this, may his prayer and his blessing keep us forever, amen.

### The wife of Badala Ma‘at is abducted and her property ransacked

75 And again listen! We will tell you the miracle that our blessed father Yonas did in the land of Séme‘a while the governor of Same‘a was Gada, son of Saladawit, and in his place he appointed his brother but he himself was in Dakano. The wife of Badela Ma’at was in Tekule but her husband went to the king's court for he was in the king's army. Gada sent a message to his brother, whom he had appointed in his place, saying: “They have arrested Badala Ma‘at’s wife, herself together with her male and female servants, her farmers, both the humans and animals. Nothing of them has escaped, not even the cloth they wore nor the loincloth they were girded with”. As he was saying this, Gada’s envoy arrived and they arrested them. But this father of ours Yonas, was in the church at that time of his arrival.

Abuna Yonas comforts the victims of abduction and plunder and prays for the perpetrators 76 When Badela Ma‘at's wife heard that our father Yonas came, she went to him and embraced his feet and wept and said: “Father, we have confided in your prayer! The poor have been laid waste by violence, without their sin! Tell these stony hearts (Ezek 36:26) to leave them alone”. Our father Yonas, the lord, seer of hidden things told her: “Do not be afraid, my daughter! Be quiet! What do you know about what is on the way?”. And immediately the king’s envoys arrived and seized those wrongdoers, tied and fettered them; they oppressed them as they did themselves oppress; they stripped off their loincloths and did not leave to them even their clothes. It was fulfilled upon them the (Scripture) that says: By the measure that you measure, you will also be measured (Mt 7:2). They arrested them and brought them to the king. As they were taking them away, they told our father Yonas: “Forgive us, father, for we did not listen to what you told us: ‘My children do not oppress the poor and do not make orphans cry because the tears of the orphan will not go back empty, but we refused you. We have been doubly repaid according to our deed”.

### The wife of Badela Ma‘at pays homage to Abuna Yonas
ወብእሲቱ" ፡ ለበድለ" ፣ መዐት ፡ ተዘከረት ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ዘይቤላ ፣ ተዐገሚ ፣ ወለትየ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ተአምሪ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ፍኖት ። ወተንሥአት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወቦአት ፡ ኀበ'' ፡፣ ማኅደሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮኖስ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ባሐከ" ፣ አምሳለ" ፡ ረቢ ፡ ዘተናገርከ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ፡ ሰዐት ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ ምሴት ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይዕርብ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ኤልያ ስኑ ፣ አንተ ፣ ዳግማይ ። ወሚመ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ መጥምቀ ፡ አዶናይ ። ትዕቀ በነ" ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ዐባይ፡' ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን" ።
77 And Badela Ma‘at's wife remembered the word of the man of God that told her: “Be patient, my daughter! What do you know about what is on the way?”. She rose with the people of the country and entered the dwelling of our father Yonas and told him: “Hail to you, likeness of the teacher, what you pronounced at the sixth hour, has happened in the evening before sunset! Are you the second Elijah? Or John, the baptizer of the Lord?” May your great prayer keep us forever, amen.

### Abuna Yonas frees a captive who trusts in him
ወካዕበ ፣ ስምዑ ፡ በተሠጥዎ”' ፣ ወአጽምኡ” ፣ በለብዎ” ፣ ዘከመ ፣ አድ ኃኖ ፣ ለፄዋ ፡ ዘተሰፈዎ ። ወሀለዉ” ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በምድረ ፡ አንጎት።ፓ" ራነ ፡፣ ዘመድ ፣ እሙንቱ ። ወሶበ ፣ ስምዑ” ፣ ዜናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣
78 And again, hearken you responsively, and listen with attention to how he liberated the captive who trusted in him. There were holy people in the land of Angot: they were of a meek stock. When they heard the news of


[p. 376] {#po.53.p376}
አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፣ ተአመኑ፤ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ዐቢዮሙ ፡፣ ወንኡሶሙ ። ወይቤሉ ፡ መጠውነ ፡ ነፍሰነ' ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ ይኩነነ ፡ አበ ፡ ወኮ ናሁ ፡ ውለደ' ። ወእንዘ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዐደል ፡ ወፄ ወዎ ፡ ለ8 ፡፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ። ወጸለየ ፡ በማእ ከሎሙ፤ ፡ ዘተፄወወ፡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ አባ ፡ ለመኑ ፡ ተኀድግ ፡ ምዕቅብናከ ። አባ ፡ አባ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ለከ ፣ ወሀብኩ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ተአሚንየ ፡። በጸሎትከ ። አባ ፡ አባ ፡ ኢትኅድገኒ ፡ እኩን ፡ ተንባላዌ' ። አባ ፡ ትርከበኒ ፡ ረድኤተ ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ፍጡነ ።
this holy man, our father Yonas, they confided in his prayer, their adults and their young ones. They said: “We have offered ourselves to this blessed father of ours, Yonas, that he may be a father to us and we will become children to him”. While they were in this situation, ‘Adal people came and captured one of them and took him to their country. The captive in their midst prayed and said: “Father, father! To whom do you leave your deposit? Father, father! Did | not offer myself to you, confident in your prayer? Father, father! Do not leave me to become a Muslim! Father, let the help of your prayer reach me quickly!”.
ወዘእንበለ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ተጽዕነ ፡፣ በደመና ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብፁዓዊ" ፣ አቡነ ፡ዮናኖስ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ፣ውእቱ ፡ ዘተፄወወ፤' ። ወአኀዞ'' ፡ በእ ዴሁ ፣ ወመሠጦ ፡ እማእከሎሙ፤' ፡ ወአወፈዮ ፡ ለሰብኡ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ቀበ ጽዎ ። ዝኒ'' ፡ ዕፁብ'' ፡ ለሰሚዕ'' ። ወተስእልዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አዝማዲሁ ፣ እፎ ፡፣ ኮንከ ፡
79 Before he had finished his prayer, this blessed father of ours, Yonas mounted on a cloud and arrived at the place where this captive was. He took him by his hand and grabbed him from their midst and handed him to his folk after they had lost hope of (getting) him (back). This too is wonderful to hear. His relatives asked him and said to him: “How were you?”.

### The liberated captive recounts how Abunéa Yonas freed him

80 He recounted to them and told them: “As for me, when | was in the country of the pagans, | prayed with a steadfast heart, | called our father Yonas, saying: ‘Father, father! To whom do you leave your deposit? Did | not offer myself to you?’ As | was saying this, my father came to me riding on a bright cloud and snatched me away from the midst of the apostates and brought me to you instantly’. He described to them his aspect and the pleasantness of his word. When they heard this they marvelled and said: “Who can do like this, in these days, like this monk? To whom has such a grace been bestowed? While he was in Sarawe descending to ‘Adal, he at once brought back to us the one who had confided in him”. The young ones and the adults said this and confided in the prayer of our father Yonas. Those who heard about that mighty work that our father Yonas did, admired (saying): “We have never heard about any miracle such as this one, except the one Giyorgis rabban performed upon Giyorgis, son of Bifan. May the prayer of Giyorgis and the prayer of our father Yonas keep us, amen’.

### On a man declaring that he refuses to be Abuna Yonas’s follower
ወጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮኖስ ፡ ትዕቀበነ" ፡ አሜን" ። ካዕበ” ፣ ስምዑ ፡ ንዜንወክሙ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ አቡነ ፡፣ ዮኖስ ፣ ብፁዓዊ ። በገድል ፣ ፃማዊ ። ጣረከተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ በገራህተ ፡ ልብነ ፡ ይስዊ ። "'ወሀሎ ፡ ፣ ብእሲ ፡ እምደቂቀ ፣ ትምህርቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ የ።ኖስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፣ ዳንኤል ፡፣

ወያፈቅሮ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ፣ልቡ ፡ ወበኩሉ፤ ፡ ኅሊናሁ፤ ። ወቦ ፡ እኅወ ፡ ፡ ዘየአብያ' ፡ ለበረከተ ፡ ትምህርቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዮናስ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፣ እኀሁ ፣ ዳንኤል ፣ ለምንት ፣ ተአብያ' ፡ ለትምህርተ ፡ አቡነ ፣ ዮኖስ ፡ ይጌ ይሰከ ፡፣ ለእመ ፡፣ ኢአበይካሃ፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ ትክል ፣ ወታሰልጥ' ። ወይቤ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይትዌከፎ" ፡ ለዝ ፡ አንተ ፡ ዘትብል ፡ ወኢ ይሰምዕ ፡ ትምህርቶ"' ፡ ብየ ፡ አብ'' ፡ ካልእ'' ፡ ዘአፈቀር ፡ ወዘእሰምዕ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ወትምህርቶ ፡ ወሎቴሰ"' ፡ ኩን ፡ ወልዱ" ፡ አንተ ።
by the name Dane’el who loved our father Yonas with all his heart and with all his mind (Deut 6:5//). There was also a brother who was refusing the blessing of the teaching of our father Yonas. His brother Dane’el told him: “Why do you refuse the teaching of our father Yonas? It would be better for you if you do not refuse it, for it is said: The prayer of the righteous has power and can accomplish (Jas 5:16)”. He replied: “As for me, | do not accept what you are saying and will not listen to his teaching! | have another father whom | love, to whose word and teaching | listen. As for him, be his disciple yourselfl”.

82 As he was saying this, the anger of God was ordered and a plague entered into their village and (it went) first into the house of that man who was refusing the teaching of our father Yonas and did not spare anybody from his house except himself and his wife. Abuna Yonas appears to him and his life is spared after he professes allegiance to Abuna Yonas

83 Our father Yonas arrived riding a white horse of the Spirit and stood over him in a great awe and told him: “Do you know who | am?”. He told him: “Lord! | don’t know you, who are you?”. He told him: “I am Yonas, whose teaching you have refused to listen to”. And he told him: “Father! Have mercy on me and forgive my sin for | have done what is not profitable to me through my lack of knowledge!”. The blessed father of ours, Yonas told him: “May God forgive you for what has happened in the past, and for the future, keep the word of my teaching because it will be profitable to you and will draw you to Christ, the king”. From then on, the plague ceased and God had mercy on his people. Having heard this miracle, his brother Dane’el was full of admiration and from then on, both of them subjected themselves to the teaching of our father Yonas. The (word which) says: When he kills them, that day they will look after him and they shall come back and they will go to God (Ps 77:34). May the blessing of his prayer be with us, amen. On a woman that was kidnapped by Arabs and saved by Abuna Yonas

Masewa’e and she survived by the prayer of our father Yonas and became a great Christian. She told to the people of our father Yonas's house that he helped her by his prayer. She said: “He told me: ‘Do not be afraid my daughter! Be firm and steadfast! Do not interrupt prayer! Do not eat their food without signing yourself in the (name of the) Trinity! Do not forget the name of our Lord Jesus Christ!’. He vanished out of my sight after he told me this”. 85 One day it came to pass that there was the reaping of the harvest of the western region. The Muslims rose and prepared a ship, they climbed and went the way of the sea’s depth but the wind was not favourable to them and they stayed five months without their ship moving. The Muslims cried aloud and lost hope of surviving. They called upon the name of their prophet Mahmad, and they were put to shame by him because he failed to help them. One night she said: “A great dread came upon me because of their perishing, and | signed my face in the name of the Trinity and | prostrated and prayed according to the order of the Church, and | said: ‘Father Ewostatewos! Father Ewostatewos! Father Yonas! Father Yonas! Pray and intercede that those Muslims may not be destroyed”.
